Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the thermodynamics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 667. The moduli space has dimension at most 989 with 5 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_667(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 667. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^989(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the thermodynamics structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 871. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 900 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 871 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the thermodynamics structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 900. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
